anchored to the ground.

SOURCE CREDIT FILE: equipment/test-armor/credits.txt
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Universal LPC Test Plate Armor
================================

This equipment-only test set combines the original male Plate torso, Plate arm armor,
and male Pauldrons layers. Original pixels and coordinates are preserved. The source
layers were alpha-composited and transparently padded to the 13-column prototype grid.

Torso Plate (male)
------------------
Source files:
- torso/armour/plate/male/idle.png
- torso/armour/plate/male/walk.png

Notes:
Original by wulax; recolor by bigbeargames; color reduced to 7 colors and adapted to
v3 bases by bluecarrot16; run/jump/sit/climb/revised combat by JaidynReiman; reduced
colors to 6 based on Napsio's Vitruvian.

Authors:
Napsio (Vitruvian Studio), JaidynReiman, bluecarrot16,
Michael Whitlock (bigbeargames), Johannes Sjölund (wulax)

Licenses:
OGA-BY 3.0, CC-BY-SA 3.0, GPL 3.0

Sources:
https://opengameart.org/content/lpc-medieval-fantasy-character-sprites
https://opengameart.org/content/lpc-combat-armor-for-women

SOURCE CREDIT FILE: equipment/test-shield/credits.txt
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Universal LPC Crusader Shield

Exact source files:
- shield/crusader/bg/walk/crusader.png
- shield/crusader/fg/male/walk/crusader.png
- shield/crusader/bg/hurt/crusader.png
- shield/crusader/fg/male/hurt/crusader.png

Idle behavior:
- Crusader has no dedicated idle sheet.
- Each direction uses frame 1 (column 0) from its corresponding original walk
  row, copied unchanged into idle columns 0 and 1.
- Idle remains static; no walk animation is played.

Layer order:
- background: zPos 2, shield/crusader/bg
- male foreground: zPos 110, shield/crusader/fg/male

Authors:
- bluecarrot16
- Sander Frenken (castelonia)

License:
- OGA-BY 3.0

Source:
- https://opengameart.org/content/lpc-shields

Processing:
- Original pixels retained. Canonical coordinates are retained for back, left,
  right, and hurt.
- The complete front/down frames are translated +27px on x so the shield is
  held by the character-left hand opposite the sword.
- Transparent padding was added on the right to reach the existing LPC V1
  832-pixel sheet width.
- No redraw, resize, rotation, mirroring, recolor, or heater offsets.
